\begin{array}{l} \forall t \in [ T _ {0, i}, T _ {0} ]: \quad \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {y \neq i} \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {i} (F ^ {(t)}, X) \right] \leq O (\frac {1}{k}) \\ \forall t \in [ T _ {0, i}, T _ {0} ] \colon \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {y \neq i} \mathbb {1} _ {v _ {j, \ell} \in \mathcal {P} (X)} \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {i} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right) \right] \leq O (\frac {s}{k ^ {2}}) \\ \sum_ {t = T _ {0, i}} ^ {T _ {0}} \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {y = i} \left(1 - \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right)\right) \right] \leq O \left(\frac {s}{k} T _ {0} \Upsilon\right) + \widetilde {O} \left(\frac {1}{\eta}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

Putting these back to (D.14), we immediately conclude (using  $N_{s} \ll N$ ) that

$$
\begin{array}{l} R _ {i} ^ {(t)} \leq R _ {i} ^ {(T _ {0, i})} + \widetilde {O} \left(\eta T _ {0} \cdot \frac {s}{k ^ {2}} \sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}\right) \\ + \eta \left(O \left(\frac {s}{k} T _ {0} \Upsilon\right) + \widetilde {O} \left(\frac {1}{\eta}\right)\right) \widetilde {O} \left(\gamma + \left(\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}\right) \gamma s + \widetilde {O} \left((\sigma_ {0} \gamma k) ^ {q - 1}\right) \gamma P + (\sigma_ {0}) ^ {q - 1} \frac {s}{k}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

Hence, recalling that  $T_0 = \Theta \left( \frac{k}{\eta \sigma_0^{q-2}} \right)$  (see Claim D.11), as long as (D.13) together with  $s \leq k$  and<sup>18</sup>

$$
\frac {s}{k} \frac {k \Upsilon \gamma}{\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 2}} = \widetilde {O} \left(\sigma_ {0}\right), \quad s \Upsilon \leq O (1) \tag {D.15}
$$

Then, we also have  $R_i^{(t)} \leq \widetilde{O}(\sigma_0)$  for every  $t \in [T_{0,i}, T_i]$ .

Stage 3. At the third stage, namely when  $t \geq T_0$ , we can continue from (D.14) (and apply the naive bound  $\mathbb{1}_{y = i} \leq 1$  and  $\mathbb{1}_{v_{j,\ell} \in \mathcal{V}(X)} \leq 1$ ) to derive that

$$
\begin{array}{l} | \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t + 1)}, v _ {j, \ell} \rangle | \leq | \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {j, \ell} \rangle | + \eta \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \left(O (\gamma) + \mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} + \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0}) ^ {q - 1}\right) \left(1 - \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {y} (F ^ {(t)}, X)\right) \right] \\ + O \left(\frac {\eta N _ {s}}{k N}\right) _ {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {s}} \mathbb {E} \left[ k \mathbb {1} _ {y = i} \left(O (\gamma) + \mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} + \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0}) ^ {q - 1}\right) \left(1 - \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right)\right) \right. \\ \left. + \mathbb {1} _ {y \neq i} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} + \widetilde {O} \left(\sigma_ {0}\right) ^ {q - 1}\right) \left(1 - \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right)\right) \right] \tag {D.16} \\ \end{array}
$$

If we denote by

$$
S ^ {(t)} \stackrel {\text {d e f}} {=} \mathbb {E} _ {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} \left[ 1 - \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right) \right]
$$

$$
G _ {i} ^ {(t)} \stackrel {\text {d e f}} {=} \mathbb {E} _ {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {s}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {i = y} \cdot \left(1 - \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right)\right) \right]
$$

then we can simplify (D.16) into

$$
R _ {i} ^ {(t + 1)} \leq R _ {i} ^ {(t)} + \eta \left(S ^ {(t)} + \frac {N _ {s}}{N} G _ {i} ^ {(t)} + \frac {N _ {s}}{k N} \sum_ {i ^ {\prime} \in [ k ]} G _ {i ^ {\prime}} ^ {(t)}\right) \widetilde {O} \left(\gamma + \sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1} \gamma s + (\sigma_ {0} \gamma k) ^ {q - 1} \gamma P + (\sigma_ {0}) ^ {q - 1}\right). \tag {D.17}
$$

Recall

$$
\text {C l a i m} \quad D. 1 4 \Rightarrow \sum_ {t \geq T _ {0}} S ^ {(t)} \leq \widetilde {O} \left(\frac {k}{\eta}\right)
$$

$$
\mathrm {C l a i m D . 1 2} \Rightarrow \forall i ^ {\prime} \in [ k ] \colon \sum_ {t \geq T _ {0}} G _ {i} ^ {(t)} \leq \widetilde {O} \left(\frac {N}{\eta k \rho^ {q - 1}}\right)
$$

Putting them back to (D.17), we know that as long as<sup>19</sup>

$$
k \gamma = \widetilde {O} \left(\sigma_ {0}\right), \quad \gamma = \widetilde {O} \left(\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}\right), \quad \frac {N _ {s}}{k \rho^ {q - 1}} \leq \widetilde {O} \left(\frac {1}{\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 2}}\right) \tag {D.18}
$$

it satisfies  $R_{i}^{(t)}\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_{0})$  for all  $t\geq T_0$

![](images/f4f7694e451cd032a4f1c4c8499ca19c1febb02b9555c5a5708d58d1914632e1.jpg)

# D.4.3 View Lottery Winning

Recall  $\Lambda_{i,\ell}^{(t)}\stackrel {\mathrm{def}}{=}\max_{r\in [m]}\left[\langle w_{i,r}^{(t)},v_{i,\ell}\rangle \right]^{+}$ . Also recall

$$
\mathcal {M} \stackrel {\text {d e f}} {=} \left\{(i, \ell^ {*}) \in [ k ] \times [ 2 ] \left| \Lambda_ {i, \ell^ {*}} ^ {(0)} \geq \Lambda_ {i, 3 - \ell^ {*}} ^ {(0)} \left(\frac {S _ {i , 3 - \ell}}{S _ {i , \ell}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{q - 2}} + \frac {1}{\operatorname {p o l y l o g} (k)} \right. \right\}
$$

in which  $S_{i,\ell} = \mathbb{E}_{(X,y)\sim \mathcal{Z}_m}\left[\mathbb{1}_{y = i}\sum_{p\in P_{v_{i,\ell}}(X)}z_p^q\right]$ .

Our next lemma shows that when  $(i,\ell^{*})\in \mathcal{M}$ , we have  $\Lambda_{i,3 - \ell^{*}}^{(t)}\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_0)$ . (In other words, view  $\ell$  wins the lottery and view  $3 - \ell$  is negligible, on label  $i$ .)

Lemma D.23. Suppose Parameter  $D.1$  holds and suppose Induction Hypothesis C.3 holds for all iterations  $< t$ . Then,

$$
\forall i, \ell^ {*} \in \mathcal {M}: \qquad \Lambda_ {i, 3 - \ell^ {*}} ^ {(t)} = \max _ {r \in [ m ]} [ \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, 3 - \ell^ {*}} \rangle ] ^ {+} \leq \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0})
$$

Proof of Lemma D.23. By Claim D.7 and Claim D.8,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t + 1)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle = \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle + \eta \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {y = i} \left(V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) \pm O \big (\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} \big)\right) \left(1 - \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {i} (F ^ {(t)}, X)\right) \right. \\ \left. \pm O (1) \cdot \mathbb {1} _ {y \neq i} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} + \mathbb {1} _ {v _ {i, \ell} \in \mathcal {P} (X)} V _ {i, r, \ell} (X)\right) \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {i} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right) \right] \tag {D.19} \\ \end{array}
$$

where

$$
V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) = \sum_ {p \in \mathcal {P} _ {v _ {i, \ell}} (X)} \widetilde {\mathrm {R e L U}} ^ {\prime} (\langle w _ {i, r}, x _ {p} \rangle) z _ {p}
$$

Stage 1. In the first stage, namely when  $t \leq T_{0,i}$ , we have  $\Lambda_i^{(t)} \leq \Lambda_\varnothing^- \ll \widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{m_0}\right)$  (see Claim D.11). This implies  $\mathbf{logit}_i(F^{(t)},X) \leq O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)$  from Claim D.4. Also, we have  $\mathbf{Pr}[v_{i,\ell} \in \mathcal{P}(X) \mid y \neq i] = \frac{s}{k}$ . Thus, we can simplify (D.19) as and thus

$$
\langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t + 1)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle = \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle + \eta \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {i = y} V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) (1 - O (\frac {1}{k})) \pm O (\frac {1}{k}) \mathbb {1} _ {i \neq y} \mathbb {1} _ {v _ {i, \ell} \in \mathcal {P} (X)} V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) \pm O \big (\frac {\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3}}{k} \big) \right]
$$

Since  $N_{s}\ll N$ , we can ignore single-view data and only focus on  $(X,y)\in \mathcal{Z}_m$ . Using Induction Hypothesis C.3, we know for  $(X,y)\in \mathcal{Z}_m$

$$
V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) = \sum_ {p \in \mathcal {P} _ {v _ {i, \ell}} (X)} \widetilde {\mathsf {R e L U}} ^ {\prime} \left(\langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle z _ {p} \pm \widetilde {o} (\sigma_ {0})\right) z _ {p}
$$

and furthermore since we are in stage 1, it satisfies  $\langle w_{i,r}^{(t)},v_{i,\ell}\rangle z_p\leq O(\Lambda_\emptyset^-)\ll \varrho$  (see Claim D.11) so

$$
V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) = \frac {1}{\varrho^ {q - 1}} ([ \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle ] ^ {+}) ^ {q - 1} \sum_ {p \in \mathcal {P} _ {v _ {i, \ell}} (X)} z _ {p} ^ {q} \pm \widetilde {\cal O} (\sigma_ {0})
$$

Therefore,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t + 1)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle = \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle + \frac {\eta}{\varrho^ {q - 1}} ([ \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle ] ^ {+}) ^ {q - 1} \Big ((1 - O (\frac {1}{k})) \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {i = y} \sum_ {p \in \mathcal {P} _ {v _ {i, \ell}} (X)} z _ {p} ^ {q} \right] \pm O (\frac {s}{k ^ {2}}) \Big) \\ \pm O \big (\frac {\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3}}{k} \big) \cdot \eta \\ = \left\langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \right\rangle + \frac {\eta}{\varrho^ {q - 1}} \left(\left[ \left\langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \right\rangle \right] ^ {+}\right) ^ {q - 1} \left(1 - O \left(\frac {1}{\operatorname {p o l y l o g} (k)}\right)\right) S _ {i, \ell} \pm \widetilde {o} \left(\frac {\sigma_ {0}}{k} \eta\right) \tag {D.20} \\ \end{array}
$$

This means, if we take  $r^* = \arg \max_{r \in [m]} \left\{ \langle w_{i,r}^{(0)}, v_{i,\ell^*} \rangle \right\}$  and an arbitrary  $r \in [m]$ , we can define

$x_{t} = \langle w_{i,r^{*}}^{(t)},v_{i,\ell^{*}}\rangle \cdot (S_{i,\ell^{*}} / \varrho^{q - 1})^{\frac{1}{q - 2}}$  
$y_{t} = \max \left\{\langle w_{i,r}^{(t)},v_{i,3 - \ell^{*}}\rangle ,\sigma_{0}\right\} \cdot (S_{i,3 - \ell^{*}} / \varrho^{q - 1})^{\frac{1}{q - 2}}$

Then by (D.20) we know

$$
x _ {t + 1} \geq x _ {t} + \eta C _ {t} x _ {t} ^ {q - 1} \quad \text {a n d} \quad y _ {t + 1} \leq y _ {t} + \eta S C _ {t} y _ {t} ^ {q - 1}
$$

for some  $C_t = 1 - O\left(\frac{1}{\text{polylog}(k)}\right) \in [0.9, 1]$  (where the value in the big  $O$  notion can vary per iteration) and constant  $S = \frac{S_{i,3 - \ell^*}}{S_{i,\ell^*}}\left(1 + \frac{1}{\text{polylog}(k)}\right)$  that does not depend on  $t$ .

Now, since  $\Lambda_{i,\ell^{*}}^{(0)}\geq \Lambda_{i,3 - \ell^{*}}^{(0)}\left(\frac{S_{i,3 - \ell^{*}}}{S_{i,\ell^{*}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{q - 2}} + \frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}(k)}$  implies  $x_0\geq y_0S^{\frac{1}{q - 2}}(1 + \frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}(k)})$  and we can apply Lemma D.19 to derive that

- when  $\langle w_{i,r^*}^{(t)}, v_{i,\ell^*} \rangle$  reaches  $\widetilde{\Omega}(1)$ , which necessarily is an iteration  $t \geq T_{0,i}$ , we still have that

$$
y _ {t} \leq \widetilde {O} (y _ {0}) \Rightarrow \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, 3 - \ell^ {*}} \rangle \leq \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0})
$$

(This uses  $|\langle w_{i,r}^{(0)},v_{i,3 - \ell^*}\rangle |\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_0).)$  Therefore, we finished the proof that for every  $t\le T_{0,i}$ $\Lambda_{i,3 - \ell}^{(t)}\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_0)$

Stage 2. In the second stage, namely when  $t \in [T_{0,i}, T_i]$ , let us denote  $\ell = 3 - \ell^*$  for abbreviation. Suppose we want to prove  $\Lambda_{i,3 - \ell^*}^{(t + 1)} \leq \widetilde{O}(\sigma_0)$ . By Claim D.7 and Claim D.8 again (but this time only using the upper bound part),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t + 1)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle = \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle + \eta \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {y = i} \left(V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) \pm O \big (\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} \big)\right) \left(1 - \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {i} (F ^ {(t)}, X)\right) \right. \\ \left. \pm \mathbb {1} _ {y \neq i} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} + \mathbb {1} _ {v _ {i, \ell} \in \mathcal {P} (X)} V _ {i, r, \ell} (X)\right) \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {i} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right) \right] \tag {D.21} \\ \end{array}
$$

where

$$
V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) = \sum_ {p \in \mathcal {P} _ {v _ {i, \ell}} (X)} \widetilde {\mathrm {R e L U}} ^ {\prime} (\langle w _ {i, r}, x _ {p} \rangle) z _ {p}
$$

- Using Induction Hypothesis C.3 and  $\Lambda_{i,3 - \ell^{*}}^{(t)}\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_{0})$  , we know for  $(X,y)\in \mathcal{Z}_m$  , and for

$(X,y)\in \mathcal{Z}_s$  but  $y\neq i$

$$
V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) = \sum_ {p \in \mathcal {P} _ {v _ {i, \ell}} (X)} \widetilde {\mathrm {R e L U}} ^ {\prime} \left(\langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle z _ {p} \pm \widetilde {o} (\sigma_ {0})\right) z _ {p} \leq \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1})
$$

- Otherwise for  $(X, y) \sim \mathcal{Z}_s$  and  $y = i$ , we can use  $\ell = 3 - \ell^*$  and Induction Hypothesis C.3e to derive

$$
V _ {i, r, \ell} (X) = \sum_ {p \in \mathcal {P} _ {v _ {i, \ell}} (X)} \widetilde {\operatorname {R e L U}} ^ {\prime} \left(\langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, x _ {p} \rangle\right) z _ {p} \leq \widetilde {O} \left(\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}\right) \tag {D.22}
$$

- For  $(X,y) \sim \mathcal{Z}_s$  and  $y \neq i$ , we have  $\mathbf{logit}_i(F^{(t)},X) \leq O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)(1 - \mathbf{logit}_y(F^{(t)},X))$  (see Claim D.4)

Putting these back to (D.21), we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} | \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t + 1)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle | \leq | \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle | + O (\eta) \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {y = i} \left(\widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}) + O (\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3})\right) \left(1 - \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {y} (F ^ {(t)}, X)\right) \right. \\ \left. + \mathbb {1} _ {y \neq i} \widetilde {\mathcal {O}} (\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}) \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {i} (F ^ {(t)}, X) \right] \\ + O \left(\frac {\eta N _ {s}}{N}\right) \underset {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {s}} {\mathbb {E}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {y = i} \widetilde {O} \left(\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}\right) \cdot \left(1 - \operatorname {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right)\right) \right. \tag {D.23} \\ + \mathbb {1} _ {y \neq i} \frac {\mathcal {E} _ {1} + \mathcal {E} _ {3} + \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1})}{k} \Big (1 - \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {y} \big (F ^ {(t)}, X \big) \Big) \Big ] \\ \end{array}
$$

Since  $N_{s} \ll N$ , we can ignore single-view data and only focus on  $(X, y) \in \mathcal{Z}_{m}$ . Applying Claim D.17, we can conclude that

$$
| \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(t + 1)}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle | \leq | \langle w _ {i, r} ^ {(T _ {0, i})}, v _ {i, \ell} \rangle | + \eta O \left(\frac {s}{k} T _ {0} \Upsilon + \frac {T _ {0}}{k}\right) \cdot \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}) + \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0})
$$

Recall that  $T_0 = \Theta\left(\frac{k}{\eta\sigma_0^{q - 2}}\right)$  from Claim D.11, and recall we have  $s\Upsilon \leq O(1)$  (from (D.15)), we finish the proof that  $|\langle w_{i,r}^{(t + 1)},v_{i,\ell}\rangle |\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_0)$ . This means,  $\Lambda_{i,3 - \ell^*}^{(t)}\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_0)$  for every  $t\in [T_{0,i},T_i]$ .

Stage 3. At the third stage, namely when  $t \geq T_0$ , we can continue from (D.23) but this time we do not ignore single-view data, and apply the naive bound  $\mathbf{logit}_i(F^{(t)}, X) \leq 1 - \mathbf{logit}_y(F^{(t)}, X)$  for  $(X, y) \in \mathcal{Z}_m$  and  $i \neq y$ . Recall again we denote  $\ell = 3 - \ell^*$  for notational simplicity. If we denote by

$$
S ^ {(t)} \stackrel {\mathrm {d e f}} {=} \mathbb {E} _ {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {m}} \left[ 1 - \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right) \right]
$$

$$
G _ {i} ^ {(t)} \stackrel {\mathrm {d e f}} {=} \mathbb {E} _ {(X, y) \sim \mathcal {Z} _ {s}} \left[ \mathbb {1} _ {i = y} \cdot \left(1 - \mathbf {l o g i t} _ {y} \left(F ^ {(t)}, X\right)\right) \right]
$$

then we have

$$
\Lambda_ {i, 3 - \ell^ {*}} ^ {(t + 1)} \leq \Lambda_ {i, 3 - \ell^ {*}} ^ {(t)} + \widetilde {O} \left(\eta S ^ {(t)} \sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}\right) + O \left(\frac {\eta N _ {s}}{N}\right) \cdot \left(G _ {i} ^ {(t)} + \frac {\sum_ {j \in [ k ]} G _ {j} ^ {(t)}}{k}\right) \cdot \widetilde {O} \left(\sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1}\right) \tag {D.24}
$$

Recall

$$
\text {C l a i m} \quad D. 1 4 \Rightarrow \sum_ {t \geq T _ {0}} S ^ {(t)} \leq \widetilde {O} \left(\frac {k}{\eta}\right)
$$

$$
\mathrm {C l a i m D . 1 2} \Rightarrow \forall i ^ {\prime} \in [ k ] \colon \sum_ {t \geq T _ {0}} G _ {i} ^ {(t)} \leq \widetilde {O} \left(\frac {N}{\eta k \rho^ {q - 1}}\right)
$$

Putting them back to (D.24), we know that as long as  $\frac{N_s}{k\rho^{q - 1}} \leq \widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sigma_0^{q - 2}}\right)$  (already satisfied in (D.18))

and additionally $^{20}$

$$
k \sigma_ {0} ^ {q - 1} = \widetilde {O} (\sigma_ {0})
$$

we have  $\Lambda_{i,3 - \ell^{*}}^{(t + 1)}\leq \widetilde{O} (\sigma_0)$
